# DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN

	title = cosmo_admduo_events.5001.t
	desc = cosmo_admduo_events.5001.d
	picture = {
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1
			}
		}
	picture = cosmogfx_DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {	
		has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2
			}		
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2_eventPicture
	}

			}

		NOT = {

			}		
	}

	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {	
		has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_5
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_6
			}		
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_6_eventPicture
	}
	
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_admduo_events.5001.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
		has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_a			
		 }	
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1		
       }	
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2
						NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_3
			}			
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_3
       }
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_4
						NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_5
			}			
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_5
       }
		set_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_a
		
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			change_government_reform_progress = 15
			add_prestige = 5
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_II
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_I
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			} 
		}

		remove_country_modifier = having_an_affair
		
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_ruler_flag = ruler_having_affair				
	   
		}		
		}		
		
		option = {
		name = cosmo_admduo_events.5001.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_a			
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1	
						NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2
       }
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_3	
						NOT = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_4
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_4
       }
		if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1	
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2		
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_3	
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_4
			has_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_5			
		}
		clr_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_1
		clr_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_2
		clr_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_3
		clr_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_4
		clr_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_5		
       }	   
		clr_country_flag = DUO_NOVGORODIAN_ARTIST_EAST_SLAVIC_STATESMAN_a
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}

				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_artist_statesman_affair_I

				}

			add_prestige = 5

			NOT = {

			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_symptoms_tooltip
			}
			5 = { #caught
			trigger = {
				always = no
			}
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_admduo_events.5013				
				}
			}			
			80 = { #no std
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_symptoms_tooltip 
			}			
			}	   
		}		
	}		
}

			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_treasurer_captain_affair_II
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_treasurer_captain_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_treasurer_captain_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_treasurer_captain_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_treasurer_captain_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_treasurer_captain_affair_I
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			} 
		}

			
		remove_country_modifier = having_an_affair
		
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_ruler_flag = ruler_having_affair						
	   
		}		
	}	
}

					}

			}

				name = cosmo_treasurer_army_affair_III
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			}

		}

				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_prestige = 5
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_III
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_II
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_diplomat_I
				duration = 3650  
				
	
			} 
		}

			
		remove_country_modifier = having_an_affair
		
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_ruler_flag = ruler_having_affair						
	   
		}		
	}

			}

			}
			5 = { #caught

				always = no
			}

				id = cosmo_admduo_events.5703				
				}
			}			
			80 = { #no std

			}			
			}
